2025-06-18T10:55:22.6158857Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6158994Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6159811Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6160512Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6160704Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6160844Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6162230Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6163451Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6163590Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6164103Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6164840Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6165287Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6165752Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6166531Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6166994Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6167377Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6167585Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6168516Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6169635Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6169966Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6170160Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6170322Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6170870Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6171307Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6171967Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6173394Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6174505Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6175031Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6175195Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6175675Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6175995Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6176150Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6176515Z ```bash
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6177385Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6178393Z ```
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6178554Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6178710Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6179096Z ```bash
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6180388Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6181690Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6182082Z ```
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6182249Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6182494Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6182933Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6183095Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6184136Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6185086Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6185220Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6185661Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6186515Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6187438Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6188298Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6189287Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6190042Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6190718Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6191581Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6191711Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6191895Z 
2025-06-18T10:55:22.6191901Z

2025-06-18T10:55:40.6238836Z     69.### Prefect vs Prefect-Client
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6239618Z     70.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6240277Z     71.**For OT-2 systems, we recommend `prefect-client`** instead of the full `prefect` package:
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6240843Z     72.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6241673Z     73.- **prefect-client**: Minimal installation (~803KB) designed for resource-constrained environments
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6242652Z     74.- **prefect**: Full installation (~5.8MB) with CLI and server components that may not be needed on OT-2
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6243233Z     75.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6244192Z     76.The `prefect-client` package provides all the core workflow functionality you need for OT-2 automation while avoiding complex dependencies that can cause installation issues.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6245057Z     77.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6245410Z     78.## Basic Usage Example

2025-06-18T10:55:40.6285818Z     161.## System Requirements
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6286257Z     162.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6286684Z     163.- **Architecture**: ARMv7l (Raspberry Pi 3B+)
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6287200Z     164.- **Python**: 3.10+
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6287701Z     165.- **OS**: Opentrons custom firmware
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6288290Z     166.- **Network**: Internet access for initial download
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6288870Z     167.- **Storage**: ~20MB free space for all wheels
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6289362Z     168.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6289882Z     169.## Background
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6290308Z     170.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6290940Z     171.This solution addresses the complexity of installing Prefect on OT-2 systems where:
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6291503Z     172.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6292031Z     173.- Standard pip install fails due to compilation requirements
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6292695Z     174.- pendulum (Prefect dependency) requires Rust compilation 
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6293289Z     175.- ARMv7l wheels are not readily available
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6293860Z     176.- Limited system tools (no git, bash, venv)
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6294354Z     177.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6295035Z     178.The pre-built wheels in this repository eliminate these issues by providing compatible binaries.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6295632Z     179.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6295981Z     180.## Support
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6296393Z     181.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6296759Z     182.If you encounter issues:
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6297218Z     183.
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6297644Z     184.1. Check that you're on an ARMv7l system (OT-2)
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6298184Z     185.2. Verify Python 3.10 is being used
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6298745Z     186.3. Ensure network connectivity to GitHub
2025-06-18T10:55:40.6299530Z     187.4. Try the automated installer first before manual methods
2025-06-18T10:55:41.6200888Z     188.
2025-06-18T10:55:41.6201754Z     189.For additional help, refer to the repository issues or documentation.

2025-06-18T10:57:03.0527538Z       ## Key Components Explained
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0527780Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0527971Z       ### 1. Pre-built ARM Wheels
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0528207Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0528487Z       The installation uses specially built ARMv7l-compatible wheels:
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0528840Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0529180Z       - **pendulum-3.1.0**: Solves Rust compilation issues that blocked Prefect installation
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0529899Z       - **ujson-5.10.0**: Custom fallback wheel for JSON processing on ARM
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0530386Z       - **prefect-3.3.4**: Full Prefect package with flow/task decorator support
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0530832Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0531026Z       ### 2. Environment Configuration
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0531283Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0531527Z       The OT-2 requires specific PATH and PYTHONPATH settings:
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0531931Z       - Packages install to `/var/user-packages/root/.local/`
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0532275Z       - CLI tools need to be added to PATH
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0532594Z       - Python modules need PYTHONPATH configuration
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0532885Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0533072Z       ### 3. Dependency Resolution
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0533325Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0533890Z       Force-reinstalling core wheels with `--no-deps` prevents pip from attempting to compile dependencies that fail on the OT-2's limited environment.
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0534516Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0534698Z       ## Usage Examples
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0534906Z

2025-06-18T10:57:03.0535315Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0535487Z       ```python

2025-06-18T10:57:03.0535950Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0536119Z       @task
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0536318Z       def prepare_protocol():
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0536580Z           print("Initializing OT-2...")
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0536851Z           return "ready"
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0537068Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0537235Z       @task  
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0537441Z       def run_experiment(status):
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0537745Z           print(f"Running experiment with status: {status}")
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0538064Z           return "completed"
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0538285Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0538454Z       @flow
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0538645Z       def ot2_workflow():
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0538884Z           status = prepare_protocol()
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0539167Z           result = run_experiment(status)
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0539647Z           print(f"Workflow finished: {result}")
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0539939Z           return result
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0540144Z       
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0540325Z       # Run the workflow
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0540555Z       if __name__ == "__main__":
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0540793Z           ot2_workflow()
2025-06-18T10:57:03.0541145Z       ```

2025-06-18T10:59:49.9958547Z     1.# opentrons-python-packages
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9959566Z     2.A repository hosting prebuilds of Python packages suitable for installing on an Opentrons robot.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9960159Z     3.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9960532Z     4.**This repository is under construction and the code here has not yet been released.**
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9960957Z     5.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9962903Z     6.The OT-2 uses a custom Buildroot-created Linux system running on a Raspberry Pi. Because it is a custom system, it cannot use binary python packages made with Raspbian in mind; because it is an arm7hf architecture, it cannot use more general manylinux wheels made with x86-64 or arm64 in mind. Since the OT-2 itself does not have a compiler, we use this repository to provide prebuilt python wheels that are installable on an OT-2 by using a [PEP-503](https://peps.python.org/pep-0503) simple package index.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9964774Z     7.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9965087Z     8.Any package identified here can be installed on an OT-2 through `pip`.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9965462Z     9.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9965655Z     10.## Quick Installation
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9965888Z     11.

2025-06-18T10:59:49.9986517Z     44.## Requesting Packages
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9986940Z     45.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9988703Z     46.Please open an issue if there is a Python package that you want that is not present, or if there is a specific version of a Python package that the index does not have. Please also feel free to open a pull request to add it! Before requesting a package or new version, please try installing it on an OT-2 first; only packages with native code components need to be here, since pure-python packages are installable from PyPI.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9990436Z     47.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9991459Z     48.Be aware that the OT-2 system can't always handle the addition of a specific package. For instance, the package might be a thin wrapper around a system library that is not available on the OT-2; or it might have a toolchain that does not allow cross-compilation with the tools we have available.
2025-06-18T10:59:49.9992533Z     49.

2025-06-18T11:01:10.2102631Z   +### 1. Pre-built ARM Wheels
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2102858Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2103136Z   +The installation uses specially built ARMv7l-compatible wheels:
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2103495Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2103827Z   +- **pendulum-3.1.0**: Solves Rust compilation issues that blocked Prefect installation

2025-06-18T11:01:10.2105157Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2105341Z   +### 2. Environment Configuration
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2105604Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2105853Z   +The OT-2 requires specific PATH and PYTHONPATH settings:
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2106253Z   +- Packages install to `/var/user-packages/root/.local/`
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2106599Z   +- CLI tools need to be added to PATH
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2106905Z   +- Python modules need PYTHONPATH configuration
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2107198Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2107383Z   +### 3. Dependency Resolution
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2107619Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2108190Z   +Force-reinstalling core wheels with `--no-deps` prevents pip from attempting to compile dependencies that fail on the OT-2's limited environment.
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2108823Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2108997Z   +## Usage Examples

2025-06-18T11:01:10.2157295Z   +
2025-06-18T11:01:10.2157554Z   +- **Environment**: Debian-based OT-2 firmware with Python 3.10
